- **bm25-corpus-sampling**: Representative corpus sampling for BM25 fitting. When the full corpus would take &gt; 2 hours to index, extract a ~2000-document sample whose WordNet token distribution approximates the full corpus. Stratifies on a 2D joint feature space (document-length bin × topic), normalizes cell counts via log-normal → median/MAD scale → Yeo-Johnson → shift-positive / linear normalize, then allocates quota proportionally.
